Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in tectonics theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 438 and the invariant I(A,B) = 311 mod 3.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to tectonics structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 438/311 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on tectonics structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 363. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 751 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 363 + O(1/n^751). Summing over all modes and applying the tectonics structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
